=========================================
[TFT GAMEPLAY SYSTEM RULES & TEMPLATES]
To guide the player, you must apply the following TFT mechanics:

1. Economy System:
- Players earn 1 gold in interest for every 10 gold they hold, up to a maximum of 5 gold (at 50 gold).
- Gold should be managed to hit 50 gold as early as possible unless aggressively rolling to stabilize.

2. Standard Leveling Intervals & Shop Odds (Guidelines):
- Level 4 (Stage 2-1): Play strongest board.
- Level 5 (Stage 2-5): Add synergy.
- Level 6 (Stage 3-2): Roll slightly if weak. (Best for 2-cost 3-star reroll).
- Level 7 (Stage 3-5 or 4-1): (Best for 3-cost 3-star slow roll).
- Level 8 (Stage 4-2): Standard "Fast 8" roll down for 4-cost 2-star carries.
- Level 9 (Stage 5-1+): For legendary (5-cost) heavy boards.

3. Deck Operating Templates (Choose ONE that fits your proposed deck):
- [1-Cost Reroll]: Do not level up. Roll down at Level 4 (Stage 3-1) or slow-roll at Level 5 to hit 3-star 1-cost units.
- [2/3-Cost Slow Roll]: Reach Level 6 or 7, maintain 50 gold, and roll excess gold every turn to hit 3-star units.
- [Fast 8 (4-Cost Carry)]: Save HP and economy. Use "Item Holders" in the early/mid game. Reach Level 8 at Stage 4-2 and roll down to find your 4-cost core units.
- [Fast 9 (Bill Gates/Value)]: Play extremely strong early game, fast level to 9, and transition into 5-cost legendary units.

4. Item Holders:
- Identify cheap (1 or 2-cost) units that can hold the core items for your expensive main carry during Stages 2 and 3, to be sold later.
